## PAY-0722 delivered — actual target capability admission preparation

- **Why:** PAY-0721 verified target SDK cross-compilation of an ephemeral P-256 EVP sign/verify binary, but target runtime, protected signer key, entropy, independent #43 ledger freshness and power-cut behavior remain unproven. `openssl-util` is a command-line tool, not protected key custody. Ruijie MT7621 Full ImageBuilder does **not request it**.
- **Off-device probe, NOT installed in rootfs:** `tools/v060_p256_physical_runtime_probe.sh` initial `36ca5d16c28b2775b2550b16a12a5a780038e994`, canonical OpenWrt release `cc83ca5d07eed860bd5be024ab49db853663308f`, private 0500 same-UID single-link executable guard `0e92299bae5a0d7bc559bb13e4b3d8d23ecd5437`. Requires explicit `--operator-isolated-target`, exact OpenWrt 25.12 release and supported architecture, a manually selected `/tmp/blaze-v2-p256-probe-*` candidate, specified lowercase SHA256 and a bounded `timeout`. Runs the existing PAY-0721 **ephemeral in-memory-only nonfinancial crypto probe** if provided by the device owner; suppresses unexpected output and returns NO production authorization regardless of primitive success.
- **CI safeguard:** `tests/v060_p256_physical_runtime_probe_policy.sh` `98ff91ee7c2b2331fe3c5f8a34aaa10255b76289`, 0500 hardening `f39e013954c2a3acef676e80196f8b64036c0b4d` checks a normal non-OpenWrt runner and unattended invocation reject; also executes existing signed OpenWrt Vendo `member_v2_*` deny-before-heartbeat/paid-effect contract. `.github/workflows/blazepwifi-build.yml` `4e3e8a99015ad11aff8ad665d29a920678d3cea6` adds named PAY-0722 step.
- **Protected signer design/NO-GO:** `docs/V2_TARGET_SIGNER_CUSTODY_ADMISSION.md` `57cdae5fa0028fc2b23752293b38973d479a5fa1`, operator private mode `19efde8b53fe66d506d778d9e68db3f5c6c0b56d`. Per-target Ruijie/x86/OrangePi signing capabilities remain unverified on real hardware. A TPM2/secure-element signing provider is *candidate only if physically present and validated*, never assumed. No private key in Git, CI, release images, Windows or operator scripts; pinned client public SPKI must be provisioned out of band and not rotated while pending receipts.
- **No physical test performed:** connected GitHub Actions runs on hosted CI, not the owner's target hardware. Even a future physical P256 primitive PASS proves neither server key custody nor an independent rollback witness (#43), a live v2 payment endpoint, or physical power-cut results (#34). `cgi-bin/vendo` **continues refusing `member_v2_*` before heartbeat and all financial writes**, and Windows can never use this tool to clear pending money.
